{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c79baf497a5797f8c7cfa3a6c1d2f0","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_7z0mut7r0uxt_1","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/process_seismic.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nSeismic phase association script using SeisBench for picking and custom association.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom obspy import read\nfrom obspy.geodetics import gps2dist_azimuth, kilometers2degrees\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nfrom scipy.cluster.hierarchy import fcluster, linkage\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Load data\nprint(\"Loading waveform data...\")\nst = read(\"/root/data/wave.mseed\")\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n# Load station data\nprint(\"Loading station data...\")\nstations_df = pd.read_csv(\"/root/data/stations.csv\")\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n# Velocity model\nVP = 6.0  # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\n# Load SeisBench model\nprint(\"\\nLoading SeisBench model for phase picking...\")\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\n\n# Use PhaseNet which is faster and well-suited for this task\npicker = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n# Group traces by station for picking\ntraces_by_station = {}\nfor trace in st:\n    net = trace.stats.network\n    sta = trace.stats.station\n    loc = trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else \"\"\n    key = f\"{net}.{sta}.{loc}\"\n    if key not in traces_by_station:\n        traces_by_station[key] = []\n    traces_by_station[key].append(trace)\n\nprint(f\"Found {len(traces_by_station)} unique station-location combinations\")\n\n# Process each station\nall_picks = []\nprint(\"\\nPicking P and S phases...\")\nfor i, (key, traces) in enumerate(traces_by_station.items()):\n    if (i + 1) % 10 == 0:\n        print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{len(traces_by_station)} stations\")\n\n    # Merge traces into a single stream for picking\n    station_stream = traces[0].copy()\n    for t in traces[1:]:\n        station_stream += t\n\n    try:\n        # Run picker on the stream\n        picks = picker.annotate(station_stream, batch_size=32, P_threshold=0.1, S_threshold=0.1)\n\n        for pick in picks:\n            # Get station info from the stream\n            net = station_stream[0].stats.network\n            sta = station_stream[0].stats.station\n            loc = station_stream[0].stats.location if station_stream[0].stats.location else \"\"\n\n            all_picks.append({\n                'network': net,\n                'station': sta,\n                'location': loc,\n                'phase': pick.phase_hint,\n                'time': pick.time.datetime,\n            })\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  Warning: Error processing {key}: {e}\")\n        continue\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(all_picks)}\")\n\n# Create picks DataFrame\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(all_picks)\nif len(picks_df) == 0:\n    print(\"No picks generated. Exiting.\")\n    exit(1)\n\nprint(f\"P picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'])}\")\nprint(f\"S picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'S'])}\")\n\n# Phase association\nprint(\"\\nRunning phase association...\")\n\n# Get station locations (use mean for stations with multiple channels)\nstation_locs = {}\nfor _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n    sta = row['station']\n    if sta not in station_locs:\n        station_locs[sta] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n            'elevation': row['elevation_m']\n        }\n\n# Estimate origin times using P-S time differences\nprint(\"Estimating origin times from P-S pairs...\")\norigin_estimates = []\n\nfor sta, loc_info in station_locs.items():\n    sta_picks = picks_df[picks_df['station'] == sta]\n    p_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'P']['time'].tolist()\n    s_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'S']['time'].tolist()\n\n    # Estimate origin time from each P-S pair\n    for pt in p_times:\n        for st in s_times:\n            if st > pt:\n                ps_diff = (st - pt).total_seconds()\n                # S-P time = distance * (1/VS - 1/VP)\n                # distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)\n                distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)  # km\n\n                # Estimate origin time: origin = P_time - distance/VP\n                origin_est = pt - timedelta(seconds=distance/VP)\n                origin_estimates.append({\n                    'origin_time': origin_est,\n                    'distance': distance,\n                    'station': sta,\n                    'p_time': pt,\n                    's_time': st\n                })\n\nprint(f\"Generated {len(origin_estimates)} origin time estimates\")\n\n# If we have origin estimates, cluster them\nif len(origin_estimates) > 0:\n    origin_est_df = pd.DataFrame(origin_estimates)\n    origin_times = sorted(origin_est_df['origin_time'].tolist())\n\n    # Cluster origin times\n    if len(origin_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - origin_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in origin_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=15, criterion='distance')  # 15s window for clustering\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    # Get cluster representative times (use median)\n    events_list = []\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [origin_times[i] for i in range(len(origin_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\nelse:\n    # Fallback: use simple time clustering of picks\n    print(\"No P-S pairs found, using simple time clustering...\")\n    all_times = sorted(picks_df['time'].unique())\n\n    if len(all_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - all_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in all_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=30, criterion='distance')\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    events_list = []\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [all_times[i] for i in range(len(all_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Filter events to be within the data time range\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    data_start = picks_df['time'].min()\n    data_end = picks_df['time'].max()\n    # Keep events that could reasonably be within the data (allow some buffer)\n    events_list = [t for t in events_list if data_start - timedelta(seconds=60) <= t <= data_end]\n\nprint(f\"\\nFound {len(events_list)} earthquake events\")\n\n# Write results\nresults_df = pd.DataFrame({\n    'time': [t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S') for t in events_list]\n})\nresults_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"\\nResults written to /root/results.csv\")\n\n# Print events\nprint(\"\\nDetected earthquake events:\")\nfor i, t in enumerate(events_list):\n    print(f\"  Event {i+1}: {t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')}\")\n"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2.1","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":348,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":179,"cache_read_input_tokens":30358},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"eed325dd-11c3-4a5c-ac3a-27f7f10ef346","uuid":"e156fb04-d4bd-4ade-a7a4-b462b6d6b54d"}

Get station info from the stream\n            net = station_stream[0].stats.network\n            sta = station_stream[0].stats.station\n            loc = station_stream[0].stats.location if station_stream[0].stats.location else \"\"\n\n            all_picks.append({\n                'network': net,\n                'station': sta,\n                'location': loc,\n                'phase': pick.phase_hint,\n                'time': pick.time.datetime,\n            })\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  Warning: Error processing {key}: {e}\")\n        continue\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(all_picks)}\")\n\n# Create picks DataFrame\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(all_picks)\nif len(picks_df) == 0:\n    print(\"No picks generated. Initialize GaMMA associator\n    associator = PhaseAssociator(\n        method='gamma',\n        velmod={'vp': 6.0, 'vs': 6.0/1.75},\n        gamma=[0.2, 0.4],  # Use reasonable gamma parameters\n        resample=[2.0, 10.0]\n    )\n\n    # Run association\n    events = associator.associate(picks_df, stations_meta_df)\n    events_list = events['time'].tolist() if len(events) > 0 else []\n\nexcept Exception as e:\n    print(f\"GaMMA association failed: {e}\")\n    print(\"Trying simple association approach...\")\n\n    # Simple association: group picks by time\n    # For each station, pair P and S picks\n    events_list = []\n\n    # Group picks by station\n    station_picks = {}\n    for _, pick in picks_df.iterrows():\n        sta = pick['station']\n        if sta not in station_picks:\n            station_picks[sta] = {'P': [], 'S': []}\n        station_picks[sta][pick['phase']].append(pick['time'])\n\n    # Simple approach: find clusters of picks within time window\n    all_times = sorted(picks_df['time'].unique())\n    if len(all_times) > 0:\n        # Cluster picks that occur within 30 seconds of each other\n        from scipy.cluster.hierarchy import fcluster, linkage\n        times_array = np.array([(t - all_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in all_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        if len(times_array) > 1:\n            Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n            clusters = fcluster(Z, t=30, criterion='distance')\n        else:\n            clusters = [1]\n\n        # Get cluster representative times (use median)\n        for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n            cluster_times = [all_times[i] for i in range(len(all_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n            if cluster_times:\n                median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n                events_list.append(median_time)\n\n# Alternative: Simple time-window clustering\nif len(events_list) == 0 and len(picks_df) > 0:\n    print(\"Using alternative simple association...\")\n    # Group picks by approximate origin time using travel time differences\n    # Assume stations at various distances, so P-S time differences indicate distance\n\n    # Get station locations\n    station_locs = {}\n    for _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n        sta = row['station']\n        if sta not in station_locs:\n            station_locs[sta] = (row['longitude'], row['latitude'])\n\n    # For each station, calculate theoretical P-S time difference for different distances\n    # and estimate origin time\n\n    origin_estimates = []\n\n    for sta, (lon, lat) in station_locs.items():\n        sta_picks = picks_df[picks_df['station'] == sta]\n        p_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'P']['time'].tolist()\n        s_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'S']['time'].tolist()\n\n        # Estimate origin time from each P-S pair\n        for pt in p_times:\n            for st in s_times:\n                if st > pt:\n                    ps_diff = (st - pt).total_seconds()\n                    # Assuming typical local distances, estimate origin time\n                    # S-P time = (distance/vs) - (distance/vp) = distance * (1/vs - 1/vp)\n                    # distance = ps_diff / (1/vs - 1/vp)\n                    distance = ps_diff / (1/(6.0/1.75) - 1/6.0) / 1000  # km\n                    # Origin time = P_time - distance/vp\n                    origin_est = pt - timedelta(seconds=distance/6.0)\n                    origin_estimates.append(origin_est)\n\n    if len(origin_estimates) > 0:\n        # Cluster origin estimates\n        origin_times = sorted(origin_estimates)\n        if len(origin_times) > 1:\n            times_array = np.array([(t - origin_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in origin_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n            Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n            clusters = fcluster(Z, t=20, criterion='distance')  # 20s window for clustering origins\n        else:\n            clusters = [1]\n\n        # Get cluster representative times\n        events_list = []\n        for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n            cluster_times = [origin_times[i] for i in range(len(origin_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n            if cluster_times:\n                median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n                events_list.append(median_time)\n\n        events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Final results\nprint(f\"\\nFound {len(events_list)} earthquake events\")\n\n# Write results\nresults_df = pd.DataFrame({\n    'time': [t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S') for t in events_list]\n})\nresults_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"\\nResults written to /root/results.csv\")\n\n# Print events\nprint(\"\\nDetected earthquake events:\")\nfor i, t in enumerate(events_list):\n    print(f\"  Event {i+1}: {t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')}\")\n"}}

km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\n# Load SeisBench model\nprint(\"\\nLoading SeisBench model for phase picking...\")\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\n\n# Use PhaseNet which is faster and well-suited for this task\npicker = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n# Group traces by station for picking\ntraces_by_station = {}\nfor trace in st:\n    net = trace.stats.network\n    sta = trace.stats.station\n    loc = trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else \"\"\n    key = f\"{net}.{sta}.{loc}\"\n    if key not in traces_by_station:\n        traces_by_station[key] = []\n    traces_by_station[key].append(trace)\n\nprint(f\"Found {len(traces_by_station)} unique station-location combinations\")\n\n# Process each station\nall_picks = []\nprint(\"\\nPicking P and S phases...\")\nfor i, (key, traces) in enumerate(traces_by_station.items()):\n    if (i + 1) % 10 == 0:\n        print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{len(traces_by_station)} stations\")\n\n    # Merge traces into a single stream for picking\n    station_stream = traces[0].copy()\n    for t in traces[1:]:\n        station_stream += t\n\n    try:\n        # Run picker on the stream\n        picks = picker.annotate(station_stream, batch_size=32, P_threshold=0.1, S_threshold=0.1)\n\n        for pick in picks:\n            # Get station info from the stream\n            net = station_stream[0].stats.network\n            sta = station_stream[0].stats.station\n            loc = station_stream[0].stats.location if station_stream[0].stats.location else \"\"\n\n            all_picks.append({\n                'network': net,\n                'station': sta,\n                'location': loc,\n                'phase': pick.phase_hint,\n                'time': pick.time.datetime,\n            })\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  Warning: Error processing {key}: {e}\")\n        continue\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(all_picks)}\")\n\n# Create picks DataFrame\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(all_picks)\nif len(picks_df) == 0:\n    print(\"No picks generated. Estimate origin time from each P-S pair\n    for pt in p_times:\n        for st in s_times:\n            if st > pt:\n                ps_diff = (st - pt).total_seconds()\n                # S-P time = distance * (1/VS - 1/VP)\n                # distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)\n                distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)  # km\n\n                # Estimate origin time: origin = P_time - distance/VP\n                origin_est = pt - timedelta(seconds=distance/VP)\n                origin_estimates.append({\n                    'origin_time': origin_est,\n                    'distance': distance,\n                    'station': sta,\n                    'p_time': pt,\n                    's_time': st\n                })\n\nprint(f\"Generated {len(origin_estimates)} origin time estimates\")\n\n# If we have origin estimates, cluster them\nif len(origin_estimates) > 0:\n    origin_est_df = pd.DataFrame(origin_estimates)\n    origin_times = sorted(origin_est_df['origin_time'].tolist())\n\n    # Cluster origin times\n    if len(origin_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - origin_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in origin_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=15, criterion='distance')  # 15s window for clustering\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    # Get cluster representative times (use median)\n    events_list = []\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [origin_times[i] for i in range(len(origin_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\nelse:\n    # Fallback: use simple time clustering of picks\n    print(\"No P-S pairs found, using simple time clustering...\")\n    all_times = sorted(picks_df['time'].unique())\n\n    if len(all_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - all_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in all_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=30, criterion='distance')\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    events_list = []\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [all_times[i] for i in range(len(all_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Filter events to be within the data time range\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    data_start = picks_df['time'].min()\n    data_end = picks_df['time'].max()\n    # Keep events that could reasonably be within the data (allow some buffer)\n    events_list = [t for t in events_list if data_start - timedelta(seconds=60) <= t <= data_end]\n\nprint(f\"\\nFound {len(events_list)} earthquake events\")\n\n# Write results\nresults_df = pd.DataFrame({\n    'time': [t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S') for t in events_list]\n})\nresults_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"\\nResults written to /root/results.csv\")\n\n# Print events\nprint(\"\\nDetected earthquake events:\")\nfor i, t in enumerate(events_list):\n    print(f\"  Event {i+1}: {t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')}\")\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":46,"oldLines":37,"newStart":46,"newLines":27,"lines":[" "," print(f\"Found {len(traces_by_station)} unique station-location combinations\")"," ","-# Process each station","+# Process each trace individually"," all_picks = []"," print(\"\\nPicking P and S phases...\")","-for i, (key, traces) in enumerate(traces_by_station.items()):","+total_traces = len(st)","+for i, trace in enumerate(st):","     if (i + 1) % 10 == 0:","-        print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{len(traces_by_station)} stations\")","+        print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{total_traces} traces\")"," ","-    # Higher threshold to reduce false picks\nS_THRESHOLD = 0.5\n\nprint(\"\\nPicking P and S phases...\")\ntotal_traces = len(st)\n\nfor i, trace in enumerate(st):\n    if (i + 1) % 10 == 0:\n        print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{total_traces} traces\")\n\n    try:\n        stream = Stream([trace])\n\n        # pt).total_seconds()\n                # S-P time = distance * (1/VS - 1/VP)\n                # distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)\n                distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)  # km\n\n                # Estimate origin time: origin = P_time - distance/VP\n                origin_est = pt - timedelta(seconds=distance/VP)\n                origin_estimates.append({\n                    'origin_time': origin_est,\n                    'distance': distance,\n                    'station': sta,\n                    'p_time': pt,\n                    's_time': st\n                })\n\nprint(f\"Generated {len(origin_estimates)} origin time estimates\")\n\n# If we have origin estimates, cluster them\nevents_list = []\nif len(origin_estimates) > 0:\n    origin_est_df = pd.DataFrame(origin_estimates)\n    origin_times = sorted(origin_est_df['origin_time'].tolist())\n\n    # Cluster origin times\n    if len(origin_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - origin_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in origin_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=15, criterion='distance')  # 15s window for clustering\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    # Get cluster representative times (use median)\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [origin_times[i] for i in range(len(origin_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Fallback: use simple time clustering of picks if no origin estimates\nif len(events_list) == 0 and len(picks_df) > 0:\n    print(\"Using simple time clustering...\")\n    all_times = sorted(picks_df['time'].unique())\n\n    if len(all_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - all_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in all_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=30, criterion='distance')\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    events_list = []\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [all_times[i] for i in range(len(all_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Filter events to be within the data time range\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    data_start = picks_df['time'].min()\n    data_end = picks_df['time'].max()\n    events_list = [t for t in events_list if data_start - timedelta(seconds=60) <= t <= data_end]\n\nprint(f\"\\nFound {len(events_list)} earthquake events\")\n\n# Write results\nresults_df = pd.DataFrame({\n    'time': [t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S') for t in events_list]\n})\nresults_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"\\nResults written to /root/results.csv\")\n\n# Print events\nprint(\"\\nDetected earthquake events:\")\nfor i, t in enumerate(events_list):\n    print(f\"  Event {i+1}: {t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')}\")\n"}}],"model":"MiniMax-M2.1","stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":252,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":0,"cache_read_input_tokens":41851},"service_tier":"standard","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"eed325dd-11c3-4a5c-ac3a-27f7f10ef346","uuid":"a9b9fcd2-e6bd-4647-bcb0-07fb0103ce6f"}

import fcluster, linkage\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Load data\nprint(\"Loading waveform data...\")\nst = read(\"/root/data/wave.mseed\")\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n# Load station data\nprint(\"Loading station data...\")\nstations_df = pd.read_csv(\"/root/data/stations.csv\")\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n# Velocity model\nVP = 6.0  # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\n# Load SeisBench model\nprint(\"\\nLoading SeisBench model for phase picking...\")\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\n\n# Use PhaseNet which is faster  sta = trace.stats.station\n    loc = trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else \"\"\n    key = f\"{net}.{sta}.{loc}\"\n    if key not in traces_by_station:\n        traces_by_station[key] = on individual trace\n        picks = picker.annotate(trace, batch_size=32, P_threshold=0.1, S_threshold=0.1)\n\n        for pick in picks:\n            all_picks.append({\n                'network': distance/VP\n                origin_est = pt - timedelta(seconds=distance/VP)\n                origin_estimates.append({\n                    'origin_time': origin_est,\n                    'distance': distance,\n                    'station': sta,\n                    'p_time': pt,\n                    's_time': st\n                })\n\nprint(f\"Generated {len(origin_estimates)} origin time estimates\")\n\n# If we have origin estimates, cluster them\nif len(origin_estimates) > 0:\n    origin_est_df = pd.DataFrame(origin_estimates)\n    origin_times = sorted(origin_est_df['origin_time'].tolist())\n\n    # Cluster origin times\n    if len(origin_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - data\nprint(\"Loading waveform data...\")\nst = read(\"/root/data/wave.m\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\nseed\")\nprint(f# Load station data\nprint(\"Loading station data...\")\nstations_df = pd.read_csv(\"/root/data/stations.csv\")\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n# Velocity model\nVP = 6.0  # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\n# Load SeisBench model\nprint(\"\\nLoading SeisBench model for phase picking...\")\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\n\n# Use PhaseNet for phase picking\npicker = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n# Process each trace in chunks\nall_picks = []\nP_THRESHOLD = 0.3  # Lower threshold for better recall\nS_THRESHOLD = 0.3\nCHUNK_DURATION = 120  # seconds per chunk\n\nprint(\"\\nPicking P and S phases...\")\ntotal_traces = len(st)\n\nfor i, trace in enumerate(st):\n    if (i + 1) % 10 == 0:\n        print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{total_traces} traces\")\n\n    try:\n        start_time = trace.stats.starttime\n        end_time = trace.stats.endtime\n        duration = end_time - start_time\n\n        # Process in chunks\n        chunk_start = start_time\n        while chunk_start < end_time:\n            chunk_end = min(chunk_start + CHUNK_DURATION, end_time)\n\n            if chunk_end - chunk_start < 30:  # Skip very short chunks\n                break\n\n            # Slice the trace\n            sliced = trace.slice(starttime=chunk_start, endtime=chunk_end)\n\n            # Skip if too few samples\n            if sliced.stats.npts < 1000:\n                chunk_start = chunk_end\n                continue\n\n            stream = Stream([sliced])\n\n            # Get annotations\n            annotations = picker.annotate(stream, batch_size=1,\n                                           P_threshold=P_THRESHOLD, S_threshold=S_THRESHOLD)\n\n            # Get P picks\n            p_picks = picker.picks_from_annotations(annotations, threshold=P_THRESHOLD, phase='P')\n            for pick in p_picks:\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'network': trace.stats.network,\n                    'station': trace.stats.station,\n                    'location': trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else \"\",\n                    'phase': 'P',\n                    'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                })\n\n            # Get S picks\n            s_picks = picker.picks_from_annotations(annotations, threshold=S_THRESHOLD, phase='S')\n            for pick in s_picks:\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'network': trace.stats.network,\n                    'station': trace.stats.station,\n                    'location': trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else \"\",\n                    'phase': 'S',\n                    'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                })\n\n            chunk_start = chunk_end\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        continue\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(all_picks)}\")\n\n# Create picks DataFrame\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(all_picks)\nif len(picks_df) == 0:\n    print(\"No picks generated. Exiting.\")\n    exit(1)\n\nprint(f\"P picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'])}\")\nprint(f\"S picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'S'])}\")\n\n# Phase association\nprint(\"\\nRunning phase association...\")\n\n# Get station locations\nstation_locs = {}\nfor _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n    sta = row['station']\n    if sta not in station_locs:\n        station_locs[sta] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n        }\n\n# Estimate origin times using P-S time differences\nprint(\"Estimating origin times from P-S pairs...\")\norigin_estimates = []\n\nfor sta, loc_info in station_locs.items():\n    sta_picks = picks_df[picks_df['station'] == sta]\n    if len(sta_picks) == 0:\n        continue\n\n    p_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'P']['time'].tolist()\n    s_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'S']['time'].tolist()\n\n    # Estimate origin time from each P-S pair\n    for pt in p_times:\n        for st in s_times:\n            if st > pt:\n                ps_diff = (st - pt).total_seconds()\n                # S-P time = distance * (1/VS - 1/VP)\n                # distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)\n                distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)  # km\n\n                # Estimate origin time: origin = P_time - distance/VP\n                origin_est = pt - timedelta(seconds=distance/VP)\n                origin_estimates.append({\n                    'origin_time': origin_est,\n                    'distance': distance,\n                    'station': sta,\n                    'p_time': pt,\n                    's_time': st\n                })\n\nprint(f\"Generated {len(origin_estimates)} origin time estimates\")\n\n# If we have origin estimates, cluster them\nevents_list = []\nif len(origin_estimates) > 0:\n    origin_est_df = pd.DataFrame(origin_estimates)\n    origin_times = sorted(origin_est_df['origin_time'].tolist())\n\n    # Cluster origin times\n    if len(origin_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - origin_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in origin_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=15, criterion='distance')  # 15s window for clustering\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    # Get cluster representative times (use median)\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [origin_times[i] for i in range(len(origin_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Fallback: use simple time clustering of picks if no origin estimates\nif len(events_list) == 0 and len(picks_df) > 0:\n    print(\"Using simple time clustering...\")\n    all_times = sorted(picks_df['time'].unique())\n\n    if len(all_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - all_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in all_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=30, criterion='distance')\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    events_list = []\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [all_times[i] for i in range(len(all_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Filter events to be within the data time range\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    data_start = picks_df['time'].min()\n    data_end = picks_df['time'].max()\n    events_list = [t for t in events_list if data_start - timedelta(seconds=60) <= t <= data_end]\n\nprint(f\"\\nFound {len(events_list)} earthquake events\")\n\n# Write results\nresults_df = pd.DataFrame({\n    'time': [t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S') for t in events_list]\n})\nresults_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"\\nResults written to /root/results.csv\")\n\n# Print events\nprint(\"\\nDetected earthquake events:\")\nfor i, t in start_time\n\n        # Process in chunks\n        chunk_start = start_time\n        while chunk_start < end_time:\n            chunk_end = min(chunk_start + CHUNK_DURATION, end_time)\n\n            if chunk_end - chunk_start < 30:  # Skip very short chunks\n                break\n\n            # Slice the trace\n            sliced = trace.slice(starttime=chunk_start, endtime=chunk_end)\n\n            # Skip if too few samples\n            if sliced.stats.npts < 1000:\n                chunk_start = chunk_end\n                continue\n\n            stream = Stream([sliced])\n\n            # Get annotations\n            annotations = picker.annotate(stream, batch_size=1,\n                                           P_threshold=P_THRESHOLD, S_threshold=S_THRESHOLD)\n\n            # Get P picks\n            p_picks = picker.picks_from_annotations(annotations, threshold=P_THRESHOLD, phase='P')\n            for pick in p_picks:\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'network': trace.stats.network,\n                    'station': trace.stats.station,\n                    'location': trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else \"\",\n                    'phase': 'P',\n                    'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                })\n\n            # Get S picks\n            s_picks = picker.picks_from_annotations(annotations, threshold=S_THRESHOLD, phase='S')\n            for pick in s_picks:\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'network': trace.stats.network,\n                    'station': trace.stats.station,\n                    'location': trace.stats.location if trace.stats.location else \"\",\n                    'phase': 'S',\n                    'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                })\n\n            chunk_start = chunk_end\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        continue\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(all_picks)}\")\n\n# Create picks DataFrame\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(all_picks)\nif len(picks_df) == 0:\n    print(\"No picks generated. pt).total_seconds()\n                # S-P time = distance * (1/VS - 1/VP)\n                # distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)\n                distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)  # km\n\n                # Estimate origin time: origin = P_time - distance/VP\n                origin_est = pt - timedelta(seconds=distance/VP)\n                origin_estimates.append({\n                    'origin_time': origin_est,\n                    'distance': distance,\n                    'station': sta,\n                    'p_time': pt,\n                    's_time': st\n                })\n\nprint(f\"Generated {len(origin_estimates)} origin time estimates\")\n\n# If we have origin estimates, cluster them\nevents_list = []\nif len(origin_estimates) > 0:\n    origin_est_df = pd.DataFrame(origin_estimates)\n    origin_times = sorted(origin_est_df['origin_time'].tolist())\n\n    # Cluster origin times\n    if len(origin_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - origin_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in origin_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=15, criterion='distance')  # 15s window for clustering\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    # Get cluster representative times (use median)\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [origin_times[i] for i in range(len(origin_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Fallback: use simple time clustering of picks if no origin estimates\nif len(events_list) == 0 and len(picks_df) > 0:\n    print(\"Using simple time clustering...\")\n    all_times = sorted(picks_df['time'].unique())\n\n    if len(all_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - distance/VP\n                origin_est = pt - timedelta(seconds=distance/VP)\n                origin_estimates.append({\n                    'origin_time': origin_est,\n                    'distance': distance,\n                    'station': sta,\n                    'p_time': pt,\n                    's_time': st\n                })\n\nprint(f\"Generated {len(origin_estimates)} origin time estimates\")\n\n# If we have origin estimates, cluster them\nevents_list = []\nif len(origin_estimates) > 0:\n    origin_est_df = pd.DataFrame(origin_estimates)\n    origin_times = sorted(origin_est_df['origin_time'].tolist())\n\n    # Cluster origin times\n    if len(origin_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - origin_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in origin_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=15, criterion='distance')  # 15s window for clustering\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    # Get cluster representative times (use median)\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [origin_times[i] for i in range(len(origin_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Fallback: use simple time clustering of picks if no origin estimates\nif len(events_list) == 0 and len(picks_df) > 0:\n    print(\"Using simple time clustering...\")\n    all_times = sorted(picks_df['time'].unique())\n\n    if len(all_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - all_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in all_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=30, criterion='distance')\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    events_list = []\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [all_times[i] for i in range(len(all_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Filter events to be within the data time range\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    data_start = picks_df['time'].min()\n    data_end = picks_df['time'].max()\n    events_list = [t for t in events_list if data_start - timedelta(seconds=60) <= t <= data_end]\n\nprint(f\"\\nFound {len(events_list)} earthquake events\")\n\n# Write results\nresults_df = pd.DataFrame({\n    'time': [t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S') for t in events_list]\n})\nresults_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"\\nResults written to /root/results.csv\")\n\n# Print events\nprint(\"\\nDetected earthquake events:\")\nfor i, t in enumerate(events_list):\n    print(f\"  Event {i+1}: {t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')}\")\n"}}

using only vertical components.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom obspy import read\nfrom obspy import Stream, UTCDateTime\nfrom datetime import timedelta\nfrom scipy.cluster.hierarchy import fcluster, linkage\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Load data\nprint(\"Loading waveform data...\")\nst = read(\"/root/data/wave.mseed\")\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n# Load station data\nprint(\"Loading station data...\")\nstations_df = pd.read_csv(\"/root/data/stations.csv\")\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(stations_df)} station channels\")\n\n# Velocity model\nVP = 6.0  # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\n# Load SeisBench model\nprint(\"\\nLoading SeisBench model for phase picking...\")\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\n\n# Use PhaseNet for phase picking\npicker = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n# Filter to only vertical components (Z channel) to reduce memory\nst_z = Stream()\nstations_processed = set()\nfor trace in st:\n    chan = trace.stats.channel\n    sta = trace.stats.station\n    if chan.endswith('Z') and sta not in stations_processed:\n        st_z.append(trace)\n        stations_processed.add(sta)\n\nprint(f\"Processing {len(st_z)} vertical component traces from {len(stations_processed)} stations\")\n\n# Process each trace in chunks\nall_picks = []\nP_THRESHOLD = 0.3\nS_THRESHOLD = 0.3\nCHUNK_DURATION = 120  # seconds per chunk\n\nprint(\"\\nPicking P and S phases...\")\ntotal_traces = len(st_z)\n\nfor i, trace in enumerate(st_z):\n    if (i + 1) % 5 == 0:\n        print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{total_traces} traces\")\n\n    try:\n        start_time = trace.stats.starttime\n        end_time = trace.stats.endtime\n\n        # Process in chunks\n        chunk_start = start_time\n        while chunk_start < end_time:\n            chunk_end = min(chunk_start + CHUNK_DURATION, end_time)\n\n            if chunk_end - chunk_start < 30:\n                break\n\n            # Slice the trace\n            sliced = trace.slice(starttime=chunk_start, endtime=chunk_end)\n\n            if sliced.stats.npts < 1000:\n                chunk_start = chunk_end\n                continue\n\n            stream = Stream([sliced])\n\n            # Get annotations\n            annotations = picker.annotate(stream, batch_size=1,\n                                           P_threshold=P_THRESHOLD, S_threshold=S_THRESHOLD)\n\n            # Get P picks\n            p_picks = picker.picks_from_annotations(annotations, threshold=P_THRESHOLD, phase='P')\n            for pick in p_picks:\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'network': trace.stats.network,\n                    'station': trace.stats.station,\n                    'phase': 'P',\n                    'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                })\n\n            # Get S picks\n            s_picks = picker.picks_from_annotations(annotations, threshold=S_THRESHOLD, phase='S')\n            for pick in s_picks:\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'network': trace.stats.network,\n                    'station': trace.stats.station,\n                    'phase': 'S',\n                    'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                })\n\n            chunk_start = chunk_end\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        continue\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(all_picks)}\")\n\n# Create picks DataFrame\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(all_picks)\nif len(picks_df) == 0:\n    print(\"No picks generated. Exiting.\")\n    exit(1)\n\nprint(f\"P picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'])}\")\nprint(f\"S picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'S'])}\")\n\n# Phase association\nprint(\"\\nRunning phase association...\")\n\n# Get station locations\nstation_locs = {}\nfor _, row in stations_df.iterrows():\n    sta = row['station']\n    if sta not in station_locs:\n        station_locs[sta] = {\n            'longitude': row['longitude'],\n            'latitude': row['latitude'],\n        }\n\n# Estimate origin times using P-S time differences\nprint(\"Estimating origin times from P-S pairs...\")\norigin_estimates = []\n\nfor sta, loc_info in station_locs.items():\n    sta_picks = picks_df[picks_df['station'] == sta]\n    if len(sta_picks) == 0:\n        continue\n\n    p_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'P']['time'].tolist()\n    s_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'S']['time'].tolist()\n\n    for pt in p_times:\n        for st in s_times:\n            if st > pt:\n                ps_diff = (st - pt).total_seconds()\n                distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)  # km\n                origin_est = pt - timedelta(seconds=distance/VP)\n                origin_estimates.append({\n                    'origin_time': origin_est,\n                    'station': sta,\n                })\n\nprint(f\"Generated {len(origin_estimates)} origin time estimates\")\n\n# Cluster origin times\nevents_list = []\nif len(origin_estimates) > 0:\n    origin_times = sorted([e['origin_time'] for e in origin_estimates])\n\n    if len(origin_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - origin_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in origin_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=15, criterion='distance')\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [origin_times[i] for i in range(len(origin_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Fallback\nif len(events_list) == 0 and len(picks_df) > 0:\n    print(\"Using simple time clustering...\")\n    all_times = sorted(picks_df['time'].unique())\n\n    if len(all_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - all_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in all_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=30, criterion='distance')\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    events_list = []\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [all_times[i] for i in range(len(all_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Filter\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    data_start = picks_df['time'].min()\n    data_end = picks_df['time'].max()\n    events_list = [t for t in events_list if data_start - timedelta(seconds=60) <= t <= data_end]\n\nprint(f\"\\nFound {len(events_list)} earthquake events\")\n\n# Write results\nresults_df = pd.DataFrame({\n    'time': [t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S') for t in events_list]\n})\nresults_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"\\nResults written to /root/results.csv\")\n\nprint(\"\\nDetected earthquake events:\")\nfor i, t in enumerate(events_list):\n    print(f\"  Event {i+1}: {t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')}\")\n"}}

pd.read_csv(\"/root/data/stations.csv\")\n\n# Velocity model\nVP = 6.0  # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\n# Load SeisBench model\nprint(\"\\nLoading SeisBench model for phase picking...\")\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\n\npicker = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n# Filter to only vertical components (Z channel) - one per station\nst_z = Stream()\nstations_processed = set()\nfor trace in st:\n    chan = trace.stats.channel\n    sta = trace.stats.station\n    if chan.endswith('Z') and sta not in stations_processed:\n        st_z.append(trace)\n        stations_processed.add(sta)\n\nprint(f\"Processing {len(st_z)} vertical component traces from {len(stations_processed)} stations\")\n\n# Process each trace in small chunks\nall_picks = []\nP_THRESHOLD = 0.3\nS_THRESHOLD = 0.3\nCHUNK_DURATION = 60  # 60 second chunks\n\nprint(\"\\nPicking P and S phases...\")\ntotal_traces = len(st_z)\n\nfor i, trace in enumerate(st_z):\n    if (i + 1) % 5 == 0:\n        print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{total_traces} traces\")\n        gc.collect()  # Force garbage collection\n\n    try:\n        start_time = trace.stats.starttime\n        end_time = trace.stats.endtime\n\n        chunk_start = start_time\n        while chunk_start < end_time:\n            chunk_end = min(chunk_start + CHUNK_DURATION, end_time)\n\n            if chunk_end - chunk_start < 30:\n                break\n\n            sliced = trace.slice(starttime=chunk_start, endtime=chunk_end)\n\n            if sliced.stats.npts < 3000:  # Need at least 30 seconds of data\n                chunk_start = chunk_end\n                continue\n\n            stream = Stream([sliced])\n\n            annotations = picker.annotate(stream, batch_size=1,\n                                           P_threshold=P_THRESHOLD, S_threshold=S_THRESHOLD)\n\n            p_picks = picker.picks_from_annotations(annotations, threshold=P_THRESHOLD, phase='P')\n            for pick in p_picks:\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'network': trace.stats.network,\n                    'station': trace.stats.station,\n                    'phase': 'P',\n                    'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                })\n\n            s_picks = picker.picks_from_annotations(annotations, threshold=S_THRESHOLD, phase='S')\n            for pick in s_picks:\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'network': trace.stats.network,\n                    'station': trace.stats.station,\n                    'phase': 'S',\n                    'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                })\n\n            chunk_start = chunk_end\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        continue\n\n    # Clean up\n    del trace, stream, annotations, p_picks, s_picks\n    gc.collect()\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(all_picks)}\")\n\n# Create picks DataFrame\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(all_picks)\nif len(picks_df) == 0:\n    print(\"No picks generated.\")\n    exit(1)\n\nprint(f\"P picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'])}\")\nprint(f\"S picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'S'])}\")\n\n# Phase association\nprint(\"\\nRunning phase association...\")\n\n# Estimate origin times using P-S time differences\norigin_estimates = []\n\n# Group picks by station\nstations = picks_df['station'].unique()\nfor sta in stations:\n    sta_picks = picks_df[picks_df['station'] == sta]\n    p_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'P']['time'].tolist()\n    s_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'S']['time'].tolist()\n\n    for pt in p_times:\n        for st in s_times:\n            if st > pt:\n                ps_diff = (st - pt).total_seconds()\n                if 3 < ps_diff < 60:  # Reasonable P-S time for local events (3-60s)\n                    distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)  # km\n                    origin_est = pt - timedelta(seconds=distance/VP)\n                    origin_estimates.append(origin_est)\n\nprint(f\"Generated {len(origin_estimates)} origin time estimates\")\n\n# Cluster origin times\nevents_list = []\nif len(origin_estimates) > 0:\n    origin_times = sorted(origin_estimates)\n\n    if len(origin_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - origin_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in origin_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=10, criterion='distance')  # 10 second window\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [origin_times[i] for i in range(len(origin_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Fallback\nif len(events_list) == 0:\n    print(\"Using simple pick clustering...\")\n    all_times = sorted(picks_df['time'].unique())\n\n    if len(all_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - all_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in all_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=30, criterion='distance')\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [all_times[i] for i in range(len(all_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Filter\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    data_start = picks_df['time'].min()\n    data_end = picks_df['time'].max()\n    events_list = [t for t in events_list if data_start - timedelta(seconds=60) <= t <= data_end]\n\nprint(f\"\\nFound {len(events_list)} earthquake events\")\n\n# Write results\nresults_df = pd.DataFrame({\n    'time': [t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S') for t in events_list]\n})\nresults_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"\\nResults written to picking and P-S differential for association.\nMemory-efficient approach.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom obspy import read\nfrom obspy.signal.trigger import classic_sta_lta, trigger_onset\nfrom obspy import Stream\nfrom datetime import timedelta\nfrom scipy.cluster.hierarchy import fcluster, linkage\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Load data\nprint(\"Loading waveform data...\")\nst = read(\"/root/data/wave.mseed\")\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n# Load station data\nprint(\"Loading station data...\")\nstations_df = pd.read_csv(\"/root/data/stations.csv\")\n\n# Velocity model\nVP = 6.0  # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\n# Filter to only vertical components (Z channel) - one per station\nst_z = Stream()\nstations_processed = set()\nfor trace in st:\n    chan = trace.stats.channel\n    sta = trace.stats.station\n    if chan.endswith('Z') and sta not in stations_processed:\n        st_z.append(trace)\n        stations_processed.add(sta)\n\nprint(f\"Processing {len(st_z)} vertical component traces from {len(stations_processed)} stations\")\n\n# STA/LTA parameters\nSTA = 1.0  # seconds\nLTA = 10.0  # seconds\nTHRESHOLD_ON = 4.0\nTHRESHOLD_OFF = 1.5\n\n# Process each trace\nall_picks = []\nfs = 100  # sampling rate (Hz)\n\nprint(\"\\nDetecting P and S phases using STA/LTA...\")\nfor i, trace in enumerate(st_z):\n    if (i + 1) % 5 == 0:\n        print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{len(st_z)} traces\")\n\n    try:\n        data = trace.data.astype(np.float64)\n        npts = len(data)\n\n        # Run STA/LTA\n        sta_len = int(STA * fs)\n        lta_len = int(LTA * fs)\n\n        if npts < 2 * lta_len:\n            continue\n\n        cft = classic_sta_lta(data, sta_len, lta_len)\n\n        # Find triggers\n        triggers = trigger_onset(cft, THRESHOLD_ON, THRESHOLD_OFF)\n\n        for trig_on, trig_off in triggers:\n            # Get peak time within the trigger window\n            peak_idx = np.argmax(data[trig_on:trig_off]) + trig_on\n            peak_time = trace.stats.starttime + peak_idx / fs\n\n            # Determine if P or S based on typical S-P time\n            # For local events, S-P is typically 5-30 seconds\n            all_picks.append({\n                'network': trace.stats.network,\n                'station': trace.stats.station,\n                'time': peak_time.datetime,\n            })\n    except Exception as e:\n        continue\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(all_picks)}\")\n\nif len(all_picks) == 0:\n    print(\"No picks generated.\")\n    exit(1)\n\n# Create picks DataFrame\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(all_picks)\nprint(f\"Unique pick times: {len(picks_df)}\")\n\n# Phase association using time clustering\nprint(\"\\nRunning phase association...\")\n\n# Simple clustering of picks\nall_times = sorted(picks_df['time'].unique())\n\nif len(all_times) > 1:\n    times_array = np.array([(t - all_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in all_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n    Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n    # Use 30 second window for clustering events\n    clusters = fcluster(Z, t=30, criterion='distance')\nelse:\n    clusters = [1]\n\n# Get cluster representative times (use median)\nevents_list = []\nfor cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n    cluster_times = [all_times[i] for i in range(len(all_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n    if cluster_times:\n        median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n        events_list.append(median_time)\n\nevents_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Filter events\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    data_start = picks_df['time'].min()\n    data_end = picks_df['time'].max()\n trace.slice(starttime=chunk_start, endtime=chunk_end)\n\n            if sliced.stats.npts < 3000:  # Need at least 30 seconds of data\n                chunk_start = chunk_end\n                continue\n\n            stream = Stream([sliced])\n\n            annotations = picker.annotate(stream, batch_size=1,\n                                           P_threshold=P_THRESHOLD, S_threshold=S_THRESHOLD)\n\n            p_picks = picker.picks_from_annotations(annotations, threshold=P_THRESHOLD, phase='P')\n            for pick in p_picks:\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'network': trace.stats.network,\n                    'station': trace.stats.station,\n                    'phase': 'P',\n                    'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                })\n\n            s_picks = picker.picks_from_annotations(annotations, threshold=S_THRESHOLD, phase='S')\n            for pick in s_picks:\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'network': trace.stats.network,\n                    'station': trace.stats.station,\n                    'phase': 'S',\n                    'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                })\n\n            chunk_start = chunk_end\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        continue\n\n    # Clean up\n    del trace, stream, annotations, p_picks, s_picks\n    gc.collect()\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(all_picks)}\")\n\n# Create picks DataFrame\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(all_picks)\nif len(picks_df) == 0:\n    print(\"No picks generated.\")\n    exit(1)\n\nprint(f\"P picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'])}\")\nprint(f\"S picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'S'])}\")\n\n# Phase association\nprint(\"\\nRunning phase association...\")\n\n# Estimate origin times using P-S time differences\norigin_estimates = []\n\n# Group picks by station\nstations = picks_df['station'].unique()\nfor sta in stations:\n    sta_picks = picks_df[picks_df['station'] == sta]\n    p_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'P']['time'].tolist()\n    s_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'S']['time'].tolist()\n\n    for pt in p_times:\n        for st in s_times:\n            if st > pt:\n                ps_diff = (st - pt).total_seconds()\n                if 3 < ps_diff < 60:  # Reasonable P-S time for local events (3-60s)\n                    distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)  # km\n                    origin_est = pt - timedelta(seconds=distance/VP)\n                    origin_estimates.append(origin_est)\n\nprint(f\"Generated {len(origin_estimates)} origin time estimates\")\n\n# Cluster origin times\nevents_list = []\nif len(origin_estimates) > 0:\n    origin_times = sorted(origin_estimates)\n\n    if len(origin_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - origin_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in origin_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=10, criterion='distance')  # 10 second window\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [origin_times[i] for i in range(len(origin_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Fallback\nif len(events_list) == 0:\n    print(\"Using simple pick clustering...\")\n    all_times = sorted(picks_df['time'].unique())\n\n    if len(all_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - all_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in all_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=30, criterion='distance')\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [all_times[i] for i in range(len(all_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Filter\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    data_start = picks_df['time'].min()\n    data_end = picks_df['time'].max()\n    events_list = [t for t in events_list if data_start - timedelta(seconds=60) <= t <= data_end]\n\nprint(f\"\\nFound {len(events_list)} earthquake events\")\n\n# Write results\nresults_df = pd.DataFrame({\n    'time': [t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S') for t in events_list]\n})\nresults_df.to_csv('/root/results.csv', index=False)\nprint(f\"\\nResults written to /root/results.csv\")\n\nprint(\"\\nDetected earthquake events:\")\nfor i, t in enumerate(events_list):\n    print(f\"  Event {i+1}: {t.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')}\")\n"}}

positives.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom obspy import read\nfrom obspy import Stream\nfrom datetime import timedelta\nfrom scipy.cluster.hierarchy import fcluster, linkage\nimport gc\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\n# Load data\nprint(\"Loading waveform data...\")\nst = read(\"/root/data/wave.mseed\")\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(st)} traces\")\n\n# Load station data\nprint(\"Loading station data...\")\nstations_df = pd.read_csv(\"/root/data/stations.csv\")\n\n# Velocity model\nVP = 6.0  # km/s\nVS = VP / 1.75  # km/s\n\n# Load SeisBench model\nprint(\"\\nLoading SeisBench model for phase picking...\")\nimport seisbench.models as sbm\n\npicker = sbm.PhaseNet.from_pretrained(\"original\")\n\n# Filter to only vertical components (Z channel) - one per station\nst_z = Stream()\nstations_processed = set()\nfor trace in st:\n    chan = trace.stats.channel\n    sta = trace.stats.station\n    if chan.endswith('Z') and sta not in stations_processed:\n        st_z.append(trace)\n        stations_processed.add(sta)\n\nprint(f\"Processing {len(st_z)} vertical component traces from {len(stations_processed)} stations\")\n\n# Process each trace in small chunks - higher threshold\nall_picks = []\nP_THRESHOLD = 0.5  # Higher threshold\nS_THRESHOLD = 0.5  # Higher threshold\nCHUNK_DURATION = 60\n\nprint(\"\\nPicking P and S phases...\")\ntotal_traces = len(st_z)\n\nfor i, trace in enumerate(st_z):\n    if (i + 1) % 5 == 0:\n        print(f\"  Processed {i + 1}/{total_traces} traces\")\n        gc.collect()\n\n    try:\n        start_time = trace.stats.starttime\n        end_time = trace.stats.endtime\n\n        chunk_start = start_time\n        while chunk_start < end_time:\n            chunk_end = min(chunk_start + CHUNK_DURATION, end_time)\n\n            if chunk_end - chunk_start < 30:\n                break\n\n            sliced = trace.slice(starttime=chunk_start, endtime=chunk_end)\n\n            if sliced.stats.npts < 3000:\n                chunk_start = chunk_end\n                continue\n\n            stream = Stream([sliced])\n\n            annotations = picker.annotate(stream, batch_size=1,\n                                           P_threshold=P_THRESHOLD, S_threshold=S_THRESHOLD)\n\n            p_picks = picker.picks_from_annotations(annotations, threshold=P_THRESHOLD, phase='P')\n            for pick in p_picks:\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'network': trace.stats.network,\n                    'station': trace.stats.station,\n                    'phase': 'P',\n                    'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                })\n\n            s_picks = picker.picks_from_annotations(annotations, threshold=S_THRESHOLD, phase='S')\n            for pick in s_picks:\n                all_picks.append({\n                    'network': trace.stats.network,\n                    'station': trace.stats.station,\n                    'phase': 'S',\n                    'time': pick.peak_time.datetime,\n                })\n\n            chunk_start = chunk_end\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        continue\n\n    del trace, stream, annotations, p_picks, s_picks\n    gc.collect()\n\nprint(f\"\\nTotal picks: {len(all_picks)}\")\n\n# Create picks DataFrame\npicks_df = pd.DataFrame(all_picks)\nif len(picks_df) == 0:\n    print(\"No picks generated.\")\n    exit(1)\n\nprint(f\"P picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'P'])}\")\nprint(f\"S picks: {len(picks_df[picks_df['phase'] == 'S'])}\")\n\n# Phase association\nprint(\"\\nRunning phase association...\")\n\n# Estimate origin times using P-S time differences\norigin_estimates = []\n\nstations = picks_df['station'].unique()\nfor sta in stations:\n    sta_picks = picks_df[picks_df['station'] == sta]\n    p_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'P']['time'].tolist()\n    s_times = sta_picks[sta_picks['phase'] == 'S']['time'].tolist()\n\n    for pt in p_times:\n        for st in s_times:\n            if st > pt:\n                ps_diff = (st - pt).total_seconds()\n                if 3 < ps_diff < 50:  # Reasonable P-S time\n                    distance = ps_diff / (1/VS - 1/VP)  # km\n                    origin_est = pt - timedelta(seconds=distance/VP)\n                    origin_estimates.append({\n                        'origin_time': origin_est,\n                        'station': sta,\n                    })\n\nprint(f\"Generated {len(origin_estimates)} origin time estimates\")\n\n# Cluster origin times\nevents_list = []\nif len(origin_estimates) > 0:\n    origin_times = sorted([e['origin_time'] for e in origin_estimates])\n\n    if len(origin_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - origin_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in origin_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=8, criterion='distance')  # 8 second window\n    else:\n        clusters = [1]\n\n    for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n        cluster_times = [origin_times[i] for i in range(len(origin_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n        if cluster_times:\n            median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n            events_list.append(median_time)\n\n    events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\nprint(f\"Found {len(events_list)} earthquake events before filtering\")\n\n# Filter: require at least 2 stations for each event\nif len(events_list) > 0:\n    filtered_events = []\n    for event_time in events_list:\n        # Count stations that have P-S pairs near this time\n        nearby_stations = set()\n        for est in origin_estimates:\n            if abs((est['origin_time'] - event_time).total_seconds()) < 5:\n                nearby_stations.add(est['station'])\n        if len(nearby_stations) >= 2:  # Require at least 2 stations\n            filtered_events.append(event_time)\n    events_list = filtered_events\n\nprint(f\"Found {len(events_list)} earthquake events after filtering\")\n\n# Final fallback if too few events\nif len(events_list) < 5 and len(picks_df) > 10:\n    print(\"Using simple pick clustering with lower threshold...\")\n    all_times = sorted(picks_df['time'].unique())\n\n    if len(all_times) > 1:\n        times_array = np.array([(t - all_times[0]).total_seconds() for t in all_times]).reshape(-1, 1)\n        Z = linkage(times_array, method='complete')\n        clusters = fcluster(Z, t=30, criterion='distance')\n\n        events_list = []\n        for cluster_id in np.unique(clusters):\n            cluster_times = [all_times[i] for i in range(len(all_times)) if clusters[i] == cluster_id]\n            if cluster_times:\n                median_time = cluster_times[len(cluster_times)//2]\n                events_list.append(median_time)\n\n        events_list = sorted(events_list)\n\n# Filter to data range\nif len(picks_df) > 0:\n    data_start = picks_df['time'].min()\n    data_end = picks_df['time'].max()\n    events_list = [t for t in events_list if data_start - timedelta(seconds=60) <= t <= data_end]\n\nprint(f\"\\nFinal: {len(events_list)} earthquake events\")\n\n# Write results\nresults_df = pd.DataFrame({\n    'time':
